===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 180-205
Documentation:

Method code:
180 |   void add(String newcode) {
181 |     maybeEndStatement();
182 | 
183 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
188 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
189 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
190 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
191 |       // For example: "return foo;"
192 |       append(" ");
193 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
194 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
195 |       // For example,
196 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
197 |       // is valid and should print like
198 |       // / // / /
199 |       append(" ");
200 |     } else if (c == '"' && isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
201 |       maybeInsertSpace();
202 |     }
203 | 
204 |     append(newcode);
205 |   }

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1300-1424
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1300 |   Node parseInputs() {
1301 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1302 | 
1303 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1304 |     // individual file parse trees.
1305 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1306 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1307 |     }
1308 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1309 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1310 |     }
1311 | 
1312 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1313 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1314 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1315 | 
1316 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1317 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1318 | 
1319 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1320 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1321 | 
1322 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1323 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1324 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1325 |     }
1326 | 
1327 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1328 | 
1329 |     try {
1330 |       // Parse externs sources.
1331 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1332 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1333 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1334 |           return null;
1335 |         }
1336 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1337 |       }
1338 | 
1339 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1340 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1341 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1342 |       }
1343 | 
1344 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1345 | 
1346 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1347 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1348 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1349 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1350 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1351 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1352 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1353 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1354 |           }
1355 |         }
1356 | 
1357 |         try {
1358 |           inputs =
1359 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1360 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1361 |           staleInputs = true;
1362 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1363 |           report(JSError.make(
1364 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1365 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1366 |           report(JSError.make(
1367 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1368 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1369 |           report(JSError.make(
1370 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1371 |         }
1372 | 
1373 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1374 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1375 |           return null;
1376 |         }
1377 |       }
1378 | 
1379 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1380 | 
1381 |       if (staleInputs) {
1382 |         repartitionInputs();
1383 |       }
1384 | 
1385 |       // Build the AST.
1386 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1387 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1388 |         if (n == null) {
1389 |           continue;
1390 |         }
1391 | 
1392 |         if (devMode) {
1393 |           runSanityCheck();
1394 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1395 |             return null;
1396 |           }
1397 |         }
1398 | 
1399 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1400 |         // externExportsPath.
1401 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1402 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1403 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1404 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1405 | 
1406 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1407 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1408 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1409 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1410 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1411 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1412 |         }
1413 | 
1414 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1415 |       }
1416 | 
1417 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1418 |         return null;
1419 |       }
1420 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1421 |     } finally {
1422 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1423 |     }
1424 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1240-1300
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1240 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1241 |     // unknown
1242 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1243 |       return true;
1244 |     }
1245 |     // all type
1246 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1247 |       return true;
1248 |     }
1249 |     // equality
1250 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1251 |       return true;
1252 |     }
1253 |     // unions
1254 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1255 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1256 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1257 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1258 |           return true;
1259 |         }
1260 |       }
1261 |       return false;
1262 |     }
1263 | 
1264 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1265 |     // are not equivalent.
1266 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1267 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1268 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1269 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1270 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1271 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1272 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1273 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1274 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1275 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1276 | 
1277 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1278 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1279 |     } else {
1280 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1281 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1282 |     }
1283 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1284 |       return false;
1285 |     }
1286 | 
1287 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1288 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1289 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1290 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1291 |               thatType);
1292 |     }
1293 | 
1294 |     // proxy types
1295 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1296 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1297 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1298 |     }
1299 |     return false;
1300 |   }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.replaceWithRhs(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1702-1728
Documentation:
/**
   * Replace n with a simpler expression, while preserving program
   * behavior.
   *
   * If the n's value is used, replace it with its RHS; otherwise
   * replace it with the subexpressions that have side effects.
   */
Method code:
1702 |   private void replaceWithRhs(Node parent, Node n) {
1703 |     if (valueConsumedByParent(n, parent)) {
1704 |       // parent reads from n directly; replace it with n's rhs + lhs
1705 |       // subexpressions with side effects.
1706 |       List<Node> replacements = getRhsSubexpressions(n);
1707 |       List<Node> newReplacements = Lists.newArrayList();
1708 |       for (int i = 0; i < replacements.size() - 1; i++) {
1709 |         newReplacements.addAll(getSideEffectNodes(replacements.get(i)));
1710 |       }
1711 |       Node valueExpr = replacements.get(replacements.size() - 1);
1712 |       valueExpr.detachFromParent();
1713 |       newReplacements.add(valueExpr);
1714 |       changeProxy.replaceWith(
1715 |           parent, n, collapseReplacements(newReplacements));
1716 |     } else if (n.isAssign() && !parent.isFor()) {
1717 |       // assignment appears in a RHS expression.  we have already
1718 |       // considered names in the assignment's RHS as being referenced;
1719 |       // replace the assignment with its RHS.
1720 |       // TODO(user) make the pass smarter about these cases and/or run
1721 |       // this pass and RemoveConstantExpressions together in a loop.
1722 |       Node replacement = n.getLastChild();
1723 |       replacement.detachFromParent();
1724 |       changeProxy.replaceWith(parent, n, replacement);
1725 |     } else {
1726 |       replaceTopLevelExpressionWithRhs(parent, n);
1727 |     }
1728 |   }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1974-1976
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1975 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1976 |   }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 157-261
Documentation:

Method code:
157 |     @Override
158 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
159 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
160 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
161 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
162 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
163 |         return;
164 |       }
165 | 
166 |       Definition def =
167 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
168 |       if (def != null) {
169 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
170 |         if (name != null) {
171 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
172 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
173 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
174 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
175 | 
176 |             // Unhandled complex expression
177 |             Definition unknownDef =
178 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
179 |             def = unknownDef;
180 |           }
181 | 
182 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
183 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
184 |           if (inExterns) {
185 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
186 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
187 | 
188 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
189 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
190 | 
191 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
192 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
193 |             // literal key.
194 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
195 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
196 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
197 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
198 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
199 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
200 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
201 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
202 |                   }
203 |                 }
204 |               }
205 | 
206 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
207 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
208 |               }
209 |             }
210 |           }
211 | 
212 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
213 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
214 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
215 |                                                    def,
216 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
217 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
218 |                                                    inExterns));
219 |         }
220 |       }
221 | 
222 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
223 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
224 |         if (name != null) {
225 | 
226 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
227 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
228 | 
229 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
230 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
231 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
232 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
233 | 
234 |           boolean dropStub = false;
235 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
236 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
237 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
238 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
239 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
240 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
241 |                   dropStub = true;
242 |                   break;
243 |                 }
244 |               }
245 |             }
246 |           }
247 | 
248 |           if (!dropStub) {
249 |             // Incomplete definition
250 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
251 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
252 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
253 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
254 |                                                      definition,
255 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
256 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
257 |                                                      inExterns));
258 |           }
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindReferences.maybeHiddenAlias(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 995-1004
Documentation:
/**
     * A value whose result is the return value of a function call
     * can be an alias to global object. The dependency on the call target will
     * prevent the removal of the function and its dependent values, but won't
     * prevent the alias' removal.
     */
Method code:
995 |     private boolean maybeHiddenAlias(Node n) {
996 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
997 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(n, parent)) {
998 |         Node rhs = (parent.isVar())
999 |             ? n.getFirstChild() : parent.getLastChild();
1000 |         return (rhs != null && !NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(
1001 |             rhs, NON_LOCAL_RESULT_PREDICATE));
1002 |       }
1003 |       return false;
1004 |     }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 94-155
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
94 |   @Override
95 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
96 |     SubclassRelationship relationship =
97 |         super.getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
98 |     if (relationship != null) {
99 |       return relationship;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
103 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
104 |     if (type != null) {
105 |       Node subclass = null;
106 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
107 | 
108 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
109 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
110 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
111 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
112 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
113 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
114 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
115 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
116 |           callName.isGetProp();
117 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
118 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
119 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
120 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
121 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
122 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
123 |       } else {
124 |         return null;
125 |       }
126 | 
127 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
128 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
129 |         // inheritance.
130 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
131 |           return null;
132 |         }
133 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
134 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
135 |             return null;
136 |           }
137 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
138 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
139 |         }
140 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
144 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
145 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
146 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
147 |       if (subclass != null &&
148 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
149 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
150 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
151 |       }
152 |     }
153 | 
154 |     return null;
155 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1193-1201
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1193 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1194 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1195 |       return -1;
1196 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1198 |     } else {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1200 |     }
1201 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindReferences.maybeRecordReferenceOrAlias(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,NameInformation,NameInformation)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 908-977
Documentation:

Method code:
908 |     private void maybeRecordReferenceOrAlias(
909 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
910 |         NameInformation nameInfo, NameInformation referring) {
911 |       String referringName = "";
912 |       if (referring != null) {
913 |         referringName = referring.isPrototype
914 |                       ? referring.prototypeClass
915 |                       : referring.name;
916 |       }
917 | 
918 |       String name = nameInfo.name;
919 | 
920 |       // A value whose result is the return value of a function call
921 |       // can be an alias to global object.
922 |       // Here we add an alias to the general "global" object
923 |       // to act as a placeholder for the actual (unnamed) value.
924 |       if (maybeHiddenAlias(n)) {
925 |         recordAlias(name, WINDOW);
926 |       }
927 | 
928 |       // An externally referenceable name must always be defined, so we add a
929 |       // reference to it from the global scope (a.k.a. window).
930 |       if (nameInfo.isExternallyReferenceable) {
931 |         recordReference(WINDOW, name, RefType.REGULAR);
932 |         maybeRecordAlias(name, parent, referring, referringName);
933 |         return;
934 |       }
935 | 
936 |       // An assignment implies a reference from the enclosing dependency scope.
937 |       // For example, foo references bar in: function foo() {bar=5}.
938 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(n, parent)) {
939 |         if (referring != null) {
940 |           recordReference(referringName, name, RefType.REGULAR);
941 |         }
942 |         return;
943 |       }
944 | 
945 |       if (nodesToKeep.contains(n)) {
946 |         List<NameInformation> functionScopes =
947 |             getEnclosingFunctionDependencyScope(t);
948 |         if (!functionScopes.isEmpty()) {
949 |           for (NameInformation functionScope : functionScopes) {
950 |             recordReference(functionScope.name, name, RefType.REGULAR);
951 |           }
952 |         } else {
953 |           recordReference(WINDOW, name, RefType.REGULAR);
954 |           if (referring != null) {
955 |             maybeRecordAlias(name, parent, referring, referringName);
956 |           }
957 |         }
958 |       } else if (referring != null) {
959 |         if (!maybeRecordAlias(name, parent, referring, referringName)) {
960 |           RefType depType = referring.onlyAffectsClassDef ?
961 |               RefType.INHERITANCE : RefType.REGULAR;
962 |           recordReference(referringName, name, depType);
963 |         }
964 |       } else {
965 |         // No named dependency scope found.  Unfortunately that might
966 |         // mean that the expression is a child of an function expression
967 |         // or assignment with a complex lhs.  In those cases,
968 |         // protect this node by creating a reference to WINDOW.
969 |         for (Node ancestor : n.getAncestors()) {
970 |           if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(ancestor) ||
971 |               ancestor.isFunction()) {
972 |             recordReference(WINDOW, name, RefType.REGULAR);
973 |             break;
974 |           }
975 |         }
976 |       }
977 |     }

161 |     return new FunctionType(registry, name, sourceNode,

===== M0061 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 121-160
Documentation:

Method code:
121 |   @Override
122 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
123 |     new NodeTraversal(
124 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
125 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
126 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
127 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
128 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
129 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
130 |     }
131 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
132 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
133 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
134 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
135 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
136 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
137 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
138 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
139 |     //   function f() {
140 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
141 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
142 |     //   }
143 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
144 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
145 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
146 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
147 |         .process(externs, root);
148 | 
149 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
150 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
151 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
152 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
153 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
154 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
158 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
159 |     }
160 |   }

   * Traverses a parse tree recursively.

280 |   public void traverse(Node root) {

127 |         return;
128 |       case Token.RETURN:
129 |         validateReturn(n);
130 |         return;
131 |       case Token.THROW:
132 |         validateThrow(n);
133 |         return;
134 |       case Token.TRY:
135 |         validateTry(n);
136 |         return;
137 |       case Token.BREAK:
138 |         validateBreak(n);
139 |         return;
140 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
141 |         validateContinue(n);
142 |         return;
143 |       case Token.EMPTY:
144 |         validateChildless(n);
145 |         return;
146 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
147 |         validateChildless(n);
148 |         return;
149 |       default:
150 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
151 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
152 |     }
153 |   }

===== M0089 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1000-1062
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
1000 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
1001 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1002 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
1003 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
1004 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
1005 |     }
1006 | 
1007 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
1008 |       return false;
1009 |     }
1010 | 
1011 |     if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesArgumentsCall() &&
1012 |         allArgsUnescapedLocal(callNode)) {
1013 |       return false;
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
1017 | 
1018 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1019 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1020 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1021 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1025 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1026 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1027 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1028 |         return false;
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1032 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1033 |         return false;
1034 |       }
1035 | 
1036 |       // Math.floor has no side-effects.
1037 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1038 |       // I create a definitionProvider that understands namespacing.
1039 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1040 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1041 |           return false;
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 | 
1045 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1046 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1047 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1048 |           return false;
1049 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1050 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1051 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1052 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1053 |           if (param != null &&
1054 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp())) {
1055 |             return false;
1056 |           }
1057 |         }
1058 |       }
1059 |     }
1060 | 
1061 |     return true;
1062 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstChangeProxy.replaceWith(Node,Node,List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstChangeProxy.java
Original range: 94-126
Documentation:
/**
   * Replaces a node with the provided list.
   */
Method code:
94 |   final void replaceWith(Node parent, Node node, List<Node> replacements) {
95 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(replacements, "\"replacements\" is null.");
96 | 
97 |     int size = replacements.size();
98 | 
99 |     if ((size == 1) && node.isEquivalentTo(replacements.get(0))) {
100 |       // trees are equal... don't replace
101 |       return;
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     int parentType = parent.getType();
105 | 
106 |     Preconditions.checkState(size == 1 ||
107 |         parentType == Token.BLOCK ||
108 |         parentType == Token.SCRIPT ||
109 |         parentType == Token.LABEL);
110 | 
111 |     if (parentType == Token.LABEL && size != 1) {
112 |       Node block = IR.block();
113 |       for (Node newChild : replacements) {
114 |         newChild.copyInformationFrom(node);
115 |         block.addChildToBack(newChild);
116 |       }
117 |       parent.replaceChild(node, block);
118 |     } else {
119 |       for (Node newChild : replacements) {
120 |         newChild.copyInformationFrom(node);
121 |         parent.addChildBefore(newChild, node);
122 |       }
123 |       parent.removeChild(node);
124 |     }
125 |     notifyOfRemoval(node);
126 |   }

===== M0114 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1232-1234
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1232 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1233 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1234 |   }

/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
496 |     @Override
497 |     void maybeCutLine() {
498 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
499 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
500 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
501 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
502 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
503 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
504 |           prevCutPosition = position;
505 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
506 |           lineIndex++;
507 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
508 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
509 |         } else {
510 |           startNewLine();
511 |         }
512 |       }
513 |     }

76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used, the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.

83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));

89 |     }
90 |

94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);

===== M0149 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 491-515
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
491 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
492 |     switch (n.getType()) {
493 |       case Token.STRING:
494 |       case Token.NUMBER:
495 |       case Token.NULL:
496 |       case Token.TRUE:
497 |       case Token.FALSE:
498 |         return true;
499 |       case Token.CAST:
500 |       case Token.NOT:
501 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       case Token.VOID:
503 |       case Token.NEG:
504 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
505 |       case Token.NAME:
506 |         String name = n.getString();
507 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
508 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
509 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
510 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
511 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
512 |     }
513 | 
514 |     return false;
515 |   }

===== M0157 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 470-490
Documentation:

Method code:
470 |     @Override
471 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
472 |       if (lineBreak) {
473 |         if (sawFunction) {
474 |           startNewLine();
475 |           sawFunction = false;
476 |         }
477 |       }
478 | 
479 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
480 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
481 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
482 |       int len = code.length();
483 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
484 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
485 |         if (ch == ';') {
486 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
487 |         }
488 |       }
489 |       maybeCutLine();
490 |     }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1195-1215
Documentation:

Method code:
1195 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1196 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1197 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1198 |       return s;
1199 |     }
1200 | 
1201 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1202 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1203 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1204 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1205 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1206 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1207 |       // crazy.
1208 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1209 |         sb.append(c);
1210 |       } else {
1211 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1212 |       }
1213 |     }
1214 |     return sb.toString();
1215 |   }

===== M0164 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 384-417
Documentation:

Method code:
384 |     @Override
385 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
386 |       switch (n.getType()) {
387 |         case Token.WHILE:
388 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
389 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
390 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
391 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
392 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
393 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
394 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
395 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
396 |           }
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
400 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
401 |           break;
402 | 
403 |         case Token.NAME:
404 |         case Token.STRING:
405 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
406 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
407 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
408 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
409 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
410 |           }
411 |           break;
412 | 
413 |         case Token.CAST:
414 |           parent.replaceChild(n, n.removeFirstChild());
415 |           break;
416 |       }
417 |     }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 520-548
Documentation:

Method code:
520 |     @Override
521 |     void endFile() {
522 |       super.endFile();
523 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
524 |         return;
525 |       }
526 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
527 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
528 |         append(";");
529 |         startNewLine();
530 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
531 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
532 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
533 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
534 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
535 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
536 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
537 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
538 |         lineIndex--;
539 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
540 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
541 |         append(";");
542 |         startNewLine();
543 |       } else {
544 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
545 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
546 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
547 |       }
548 |     }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2482-2491
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2482 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2483 |     int len = s.length();
2484 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2485 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2486 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2487 |         return false;
2488 |       }
2489 |     }
2490 |     return true;
2491 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 299-347
Documentation:

Method code:
299 |     @Override
300 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
301 |       if (n.isName()) {
302 |         String name = n.getString();
303 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
304 |           return;
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
308 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
309 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
310 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
311 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
312 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
313 |             expectedConst = true;
314 |           } else {
315 |             expectedConst = false;
316 | 
317 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
318 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
319 |             if (var != null) {
320 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
321 |             }
322 | 
323 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
324 |               expectedConst = true;
325 |             } else {
326 |               expectedConst = false;
327 |             }
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           if (expectedConst) {
331 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
332 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
333 |           } else {
334 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
335 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
336 |           }
337 |         }
338 | 
339 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
340 |         if (value == null) {
341 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
342 |         } else {
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
344 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1190-1197
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1190 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1191 |     int precedence = precedenceWithDefault(type);
1192 |     if (precedence != -1) {
1193 |       return precedence;
1194 |     }
1195 |     throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1196 |         Token.name(type) + " (type " + type + ")");
1197 |   }

===== M0236 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
92 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
93 |     int cycleCount = 0;
94 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
95 | 
96 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
97 |     // doesn't converge.
98 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
99 | 
100 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
101 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
102 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
103 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
104 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
105 |     }
106 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
107 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
108 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
109 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
110 |       // on the next iteration.
111 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
112 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
113 | 
114 |       workSet.remove(source);
115 | 
116 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
117 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
118 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
119 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
120 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
121 |         }
122 |       }
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
126 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
127 |   }

Original range: 1470-1490

/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompile annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1470 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1471 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1472 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1473 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1474 | 
1475 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1476 |       if (n == null) {

1479 |

1481 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1482 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1483 |         staleInputs = true;
1484 |       }
1485 |     }
1486 | 
1487 |     if (staleInputs) {
1488 |       repartitionInputs();
1489 |     }
1490 |   }

===== M0278 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
630 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
631 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
632 |     if (typeSet == null) {
633 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
634 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
638 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
639 | 
640 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
641 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
642 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }
