===== M0294 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 107-782
Documentation:

Method code:
107 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
108 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
109 |       return;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     int type = n.getType();
113 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
114 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
115 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
116 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
117 | 
118 |     // Handle all binary operators
119 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
120 |       Preconditions.checkState(
121 |           childCount == 2,
122 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
123 |           opstr, childCount);
124 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
125 | 
126 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
127 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
128 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
129 | 
130 |       if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
131 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
132 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
133 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
134 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
135 |       } else {
136 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
137 |       }
138 |       return;
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
142 | 
143 |     switch (type) {
144 |       case Token.TRY: {
145 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
146 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
147 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
148 | 
149 |         add("try");
150 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
151 | 
152 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
153 |         // isn't a catch block
154 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
155 |         if (catchblock != null) {
156 |           add(catchblock);
157 |         }
158 | 
159 |         if (childCount == 3) {
160 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
161 |           add("finally");
162 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
163 |         }
164 |         break;
165 |       }
166 | 
167 |       case Token.CATCH:
168 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
169 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
170 |         add("catch");
171 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
172 |         add("(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context,
242 |             getContextForNoInOperator(context), 0, 0);
243 |         break;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
247 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
248 |         break;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
251 |       case Token.VOID:
252 |       case Token.NOT:
253 |       case Token.BITNOT:
254 |       case Token.POS: {
255 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
256 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
257 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
258 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
259 |         break;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NEG: {
263 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
264 | 
265 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
266 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
267 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
268 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
269 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
270 |         } else {
271 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
272 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         break;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       case Token.HOOK: {
279 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
280 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
281 |         Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
282 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
283 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
284 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, rhsContext);
285 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
286 |         addExpr(last, 1, rhsContext);
287 |         break;
288 |       }
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REGEXP:
291 |         if (!first.isString() ||
292 |             !last.isString()) {
293 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
294 |         }
295 | 
296 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
297 | 
298 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
299 |         if (childCount == 2) {
300 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
301 |         } else {
302 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
303 |           add(regexp);
304 |         }
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
308 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
309 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
310 |         }
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
312 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add("(");
315 |         }
316 | 
317 |         add("function");
318 |         add(first);
319 | 
320 |         add(first.getNext());
321 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
322 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
323 | 
324 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
325 |           add(")");
326 |         }
327 |         break;
328 | 
329 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
330 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
332 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
333 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
334 | 
335 |         // Get methods are unnamed
336 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
337 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
338 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
339 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
340 |           add("get ");
341 |         } else {
342 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
344 |           add("set ");
345 |         }
346 | 
347 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
348 |         String name = n.getString();
349 |         Node fn = first;
350 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
351 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
352 | 
353 |         // Add the property name.
354 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
355 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
356 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
357 |             // Unicode escaped.
358 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
359 |           add(name);
360 |         } else {
361 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
362 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
363 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
364 |             cc.addNumber(d);
365 |           } else {
366 |             addJsString(n);
367 |           }
368 |         }
369 | 
370 |         add(parameters);
371 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
372 |         break;
373 | 
374 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
375 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
376 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
377 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
378 |         }
379 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
380 |         if (preserveBlock) {
381 |           cc.beginBlock();
382 |         }
383 | 
384 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
385 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
386 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
387 |                 !preserveBlock &&
388 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
389 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
390 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
391 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
392 | 
393 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
394 |           if (c.isVar()) {
395 |             cc.endStatement();
396 |           }
397 | 
398 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
399 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
400 |           }
401 | 
402 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
403 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
404 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
405 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
406 |           }
407 |         }
408 |         if (preserveBlock) {
409 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
410 |         }
411 |         break;
412 |       }
413 | 
414 |       case Token.FOR:
415 |         if (childCount == 4) {
416 |           add("for");
417 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
418 |           add("(");
419 |           if (first.isVar()) {
420 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
421 |           } else {
422 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
423 |           }
424 |           add(";");
425 |           add(first.getNext());
426 |           add(";");
427 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
428 |           add(")");
429 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
430 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
431 |         } else {
432 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
433 |           add("for");
434 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
435 |           add("(");
436 |           add(first);
437 |           add("in");
438 |           add(first.getNext());
439 |           add(")");
440 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
441 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
442 |         }
443 |         break;
444 | 
445 |       case Token.DO:
446 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
447 |         add("do");
448 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
449 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
450 |         add("while");
451 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
452 |         add("(");
453 |         add(last);
454 |         add(")");
455 |         cc.endStatement();
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.WHILE:
459 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
460 |         add("while");
461 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
462 |         add("(");
463 |         add(first);
464 |         add(")");
465 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
466 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
467 |         break;
468 | 
469 |       case Token.EMPTY:
470 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
474 |         Preconditions.checkState(
475 |             childCount == 2,
476 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
477 |         Preconditions.checkState(
478 |             last.isString(),
479 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
480 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
481 |         if (needsParens) {
482 |           add("(");
483 |         }
484 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
485 |         if (needsParens) {
486 |           add(")");
487 |         }
488 |         if (this.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3
489 |             && TokenStream.isKeyword(last.getString())) {
490 |           // Check for ECMASCRIPT3 keywords.
491 |           add("[");
492 |           add(last);
493 |           add("]");
494 |         } else {
495 |           add(".");
496 |           addIdentifier(last.getString());
497 |         }
498 |         break;
499 |       }
500 | 
501 |       case Token.GETELEM:
502 |         Preconditions.checkState(
503 |             childCount == 2,
504 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
505 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
506 |         add("[");
507 |         add(first.getNext());
508 |         add("]");
509 |         break;
510 | 
511 |       case Token.WITH:
512 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
513 |         add("with(");
514 |         add(first);
515 |         add(")");
516 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
517 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
518 |         break;
519 | 
520 |       case Token.INC:
521 |       case Token.DEC: {
522 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
523 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
524 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
525 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
526 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
527 |         if (postProp != 0) {
528 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
529 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
530 |         } else {
531 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
532 |           add(first);
533 |         }
534 |         break;
535 |       }
536 | 
537 |       case Token.CALL:
538 |         // We have two special cases here:
539 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
540 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
541 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
542 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
543 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
544 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
545 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
546 |         // that must be preserved.
547 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
548 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
549 |           add("(0,");
550 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
551 |           add(")");
552 |         } else {
553 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
554 |         }
555 |         add("(");
556 |         addList(first.getNext());
557 |         add(")");
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.IF:
561 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
562 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
563 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
564 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
565 |           cc.beginBlock();
566 |         }
567 | 
568 |         add("if");
569 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
570 |         add("(");
571 |         add(first);
572 |         add(")");
573 | 
574 |         if (hasElse) {
575 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
576 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
577 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
578 |           add("else");
579 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
580 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
581 |         } else {
582 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
583 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
584 |         }
585 | 
586 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
587 |           cc.endBlock();
588 |         }
589 |         break;
590 | 
591 |       case Token.NULL:
592 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
593 |         cc.addConstant("null");
594 |         break;
595 | 
596 |       case Token.THIS:
597 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
598 |         add("this");
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.FALSE:
602 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
603 |         cc.addConstant("false");
604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.TRUE:
607 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
608 |         cc.addConstant("true");
609 |         break;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
612 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
613 |         add("continue");
614 |         if (childCount == 1) {
615 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
616 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
617 |           }
618 |           add(" ");
619 |           add(first);
620 |         }
621 |         cc.endStatement();
622 |         break;
623 | 
624 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
625 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
626 |         add("debugger");
627 |         cc.endStatement();
628 |         break;
629 | 
630 |       case Token.BREAK:
631 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
632 |         add("break");
633 |         if (childCount == 1) {
634 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
635 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
636 |           }
637 |           add(" ");
638 |           add(first);
639 |         }
640 |         cc.endStatement();
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
646 |         cc.endStatement();
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.NEW:
650 |         add("new ");
651 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
652 | 
653 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
654 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
655 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
656 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
657 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
658 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
659 |         }
660 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
661 | 
662 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
663 |         Node next = first.getNext();
664 |         if (next != null) {
665 |           add("(");
666 |           addList(next);
667 |           add(")");
668 |         }
669 |         break;
670 | 
671 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
672 |         Preconditions.checkState(
673 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
674 |         addJsString(n);
675 |         break;
676 | 
677 |       case Token.STRING:
678 |         Preconditions.checkState(
679 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
680 |         addJsString(n);
681 |         break;
682 | 
683 |       case Token.DELPROP:
684 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
685 |         add("delete ");
686 |         add(first);
687 |         break;
688 | 
689 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
690 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
691 |         if (needsParens) {
692 |           add("(");
693 |         }
694 |         add("{");
695 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
696 |           if (c != first) {
697 |             cc.listSeparator();
698 |           }
699 | 
700 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
701 |             add(c);
702 |           } else {
703 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
704 |             String key = c.getString();
705 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
706 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
707 |             if (!c.isQuotedString()
708 |                 && !(languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3
709 |                     && TokenStream.isKeyword(key))
710 |                 && TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key)
711 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
712 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
713 |                 && NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
714 |               add(key);
715 |             } else {
716 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
717 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
718 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
719 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
720 |               } else {
721 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
722 |               }
723 |             }
724 |             add(":");
725 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
726 |           }
727 |         }
728 |         add("}");
729 |         if (needsParens) {
730 |           add(")");
731 |         }
732 |         break;
733 |       }
734 | 
735 |       case Token.SWITCH:
736 |         add("switch(");
737 |         add(first);
738 |         add(")");
739 |         cc.beginBlock();
740 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
741 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
742 |         break;
743 | 
744 |       case Token.CASE:
745 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
746 |         add("case ");
747 |         add(first);
748 |         addCaseBody(last);
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
752 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
753 |         add("default");
754 |         addCaseBody(first);
755 |         break;
756 | 
757 |       case Token.LABEL:
758 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
759 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
760 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
761 |         }
762 |         add(first);
763 |         add(":");
764 |         if (!last.isBlock()) {
765 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
766 |         }
767 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
768 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
769 |         break;
770 | 
771 |       case Token.CAST:
772 |         add("(");
773 |         add(first);
774 |         add(")");
775 |         break;
776 | 
777 |       default:
778 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
782 |   }

812 |     } else {

===== M0302 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 112-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
112 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
113 |     switch (n.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.VAR:
115 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
116 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |              child != null;) {
118 |           Node next = child.getNext();
119 |           declareVar(child);
120 |           child = next;
121 |         }
122 |         return;
123 | 
124 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
125 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
126 |           return;
127 |         }
128 | 
129 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
130 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
131 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
135 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
136 | 
137 |       case Token.CATCH:
138 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
140 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
141 |         // is the code block
142 | 
143 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
144 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
145 | 
146 |         declareVar(var);
147 |         scanVars(block);
148 |         return;  // only one child to scan
149 | 
150 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
151 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
152 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-642
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
571 |     if (this == that) {
572 |       return true;
573 |     }
574 | 
575 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
576 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
577 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
578 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
579 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
580 |         // with everyone.
581 |         return true;
582 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
583 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
584 |         // both unknown.
585 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
586 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
587 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
588 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
589 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
590 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
591 |         return false;
592 |       }
593 |     }
594 | 
595 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
596 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
597 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
601 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
602 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
606 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
607 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
608 |     }
609 | 
610 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
611 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
612 |       return false;
613 |     }
614 | 
615 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
616 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
617 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (isTemplateType() && that.isTemplateType()) {
621 |       return this == that;
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     // Unbox other proxies.
625 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
626 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
627 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
628 |               that, eqMethod);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
632 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
633 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
634 |           eqMethod);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
638 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
639 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
640 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
641 |     return this == that;
642 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 505-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
506 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
507 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
508 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
509 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
510 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
511 |         String inputName = input.getName();
512 | 
513 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
514 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
515 |         // will get an error down the line.
516 |         inputs.add(input);
517 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return inputs;
521 |   }

===== M0321 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node>,Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3346-3380
Documentation:
/** Checks that the scope roots marked as changed have indeed changed */
Method code:
3346 |   public static void verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node> map,
3347 |       Node main, boolean verifyUnchangedNodes,
3348 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
3349 |     // compiler is passed only to call compiler.toSource during debugging to see
3350 |     // mismatches in scopes
3351 | 
3352 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is false, we are comparing the initial AST to the
3353 |     // final AST. Don't check unmarked nodes b/c they may have been changed by
3354 |     // non-loopable passes.
3355 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is true, we are comparing the ASTs before & after
3356 |     // a pass. Check all scope roots.
3357 |     final Map<Node, Node> mtoc = map;
3358 |     final boolean checkUnchanged = verifyUnchangedNodes;
3359 |     Node clone = mtoc.get(main);
3360 |     if (main.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3361 |       Preconditions.checkState(!main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3362 |     } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3363 |       Preconditions.checkState(main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3364 |     }
3365 |     visitPreOrder(main,
3366 |         new Visitor() {
3367 |           @Override
3368 |           public void visit(Node n) {
3369 |             if (n.isFunction() && mtoc.containsKey(n)) {
3370 |               Node clone = mtoc.get(n);
3371 |               if (n.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3372 |                 Preconditions.checkState(!n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3373 |               } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3374 |                 Preconditions.checkState(n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3375 |               }
3376 |             }
3377 |           }
3378 |         },
3379 |         Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
3380 |   }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

239 |     @Override
240 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
241 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
242 |       if (n.isName()) {
243 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
244 |           return;

246 |

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 632-646
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
646 |   }

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.allResultsMatch(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1305-1322
Documentation:
/**
   * Apply the supplied predicate against
   * all possible result Nodes of the expression.
   */
Method code:
1305 |   static boolean allResultsMatch(Node n, Predicate<Node> p) {
1306 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1307 |       case Token.CAST:
1308 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getFirstChild(), p);
1309 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1310 |       case Token.COMMA:
1311 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getLastChild(), p);
1312 |       case Token.AND:
1313 |       case Token.OR:
1314 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getFirstChild(), p)
1315 |             && allResultsMatch(n.getLastChild(), p);
1316 |       case Token.HOOK:
1317 |         return allResultsMatch(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), p)
1318 |             && allResultsMatch(n.getLastChild(), p);
1319 |       default:
1320 |         return p.apply(n);
1321 |     }
1322 |   }

298 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
299 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
300 |       return;
301 |     }
302 |

304 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
305 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);

313 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);

===== M0351 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.getRhsSubexpressions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1849-1886
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a list of subexpressions that act as right hand sides.
   */
Method code:
1849 |   private List<Node> getRhsSubexpressions(Node n) {
1850 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1851 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
1852 |         // process body
1853 |         return getRhsSubexpressions(n.getFirstChild());
1854 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1855 |         // function nodes have no RHS
1856 |         return Collections.emptyList();
1857 |       case Token.NAME:
1858 |         {
1859 |           // parent is a var node.  RHS is the first child
1860 |           Node rhs = n.getFirstChild();
1861 |           if (rhs != null) {
1862 |             return Lists.newArrayList(rhs);
1863 |           } else {
1864 |             return Collections.emptyList();
1865 |           }
1866 |         }
1867 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1868 |         {
1869 |           // add LHS and RHS expressions - LHS may be a complex expression
1870 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
1871 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
1872 |           return Lists.newArrayList(lhs, rhs);
1873 |         }
1874 |       case Token.VAR:
1875 |         {
1876 |           // recurse on all children
1877 |           List<Node> nodes = Lists.newArrayList();
1878 |           for (Node child : n.children()) {
1879 |             nodes.addAll(getRhsSubexpressions(child));
1880 |           }
1881 |           return nodes;
1882 |         }
1883 |       default:
1884 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("AstChangeProxy::getRhs " + n);
1885 |     }
1886 |   }

===== M0358 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-624
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
609 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
610 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
611 |       Set<String> directives = null;
612 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
613 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
614 |         if (directives == null) {
615 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
616 |         } else {
617 |           directives.add(directive);
618 |         }
619 |       }
620 | 
621 |       if (directives != null) {
622 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
623 |       }
624 |     }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
80 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
81 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
82 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }

251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.getEnclosingFunctionDependencyScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1584-1616
Documentation:
/**
   * Get dependency scope defined by the enclosing function, or null.
   * If enclosing function is a function expression, determine scope based on
   * its parent if the parent node is a variable declaration or
   * assignment.
   */
Method code:
1584 |   private List<NameInformation> getEnclosingFunctionDependencyScope(
1585 |       NodeTraversal t) {
1586 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1587 |     if (function == null) {
1588 |       return Collections.emptyList();
1589 |     }
1590 | 
1591 |     List<NameInformation> refs = scopes.get(function);
1592 |     if (!refs.isEmpty()) {
1593 |       return refs;
1594 |     }
1595 | 
1596 |     // Function expression.  try to get a name from the parent var
1597 |     // declaration or assignment.
1598 |     Node parent = function.getParent();
1599 |     if (parent != null) {
1600 |       // Account for functions defined in the form:
1601 |       //   var a = cond ? function a() {} : function b() {};
1602 |       while (parent.isHook()) {
1603 |         parent = parent.getParent();
1604 |       }
1605 | 
1606 |       if (parent.isName()) {
1607 |         return scopes.get(parent);
1608 |       }
1609 | 
1610 |       if (parent.isAssign()) {
1611 |         return scopes.get(parent);
1612 |       }
1613 |     }
1614 | 
1615 |     return Collections.emptyList();
1616 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.referenceParentNames()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1369-1391
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds mutual references between all known global names and their parent
   * names. (e.g. between <code>a.b.c</code> and <code>a.b</code>).
   */
Method code:
1369 |   private void referenceParentNames() {
1370 |     // Duplicate set of nodes to process so we don't modify set we are
1371 |     // currently iterating over
1372 |     Set<JsName> allNamesCopy = Sets.newHashSet(allNames.values());
1373 | 
1374 |     for (JsName name : allNamesCopy) {
1375 |       String curName = name.name;
1376 |       JsName curJsName = name;
1377 |       while (curName.indexOf('.') != -1) {
1378 |         String parentName = curName.substring(0, curName.lastIndexOf('.'));
1379 |         if (!globalNames.contains(parentName)) {
1380 | 
1381 |           JsName parentJsName = getName(parentName, true);
1382 | 
1383 |           recordReference(curJsName.name, parentJsName.name, RefType.REGULAR);
1384 |           recordReference(parentJsName.name, curJsName.name, RefType.REGULAR);
1385 | 
1386 |           curJsName = parentJsName;
1387 |         }
1388 |         curName = parentName;
1389 |       }
1390 |     }
1391 |   }

===== M0409 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0420 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.valueConsumedByParent(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1799-1822
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine if the parent reads the value of a child expression
   * directly.  This is true children used in predicates, RETURN
   * statements and, RHS of variable declarations and assignments.
   *
   * In the case of:
   * if (a) b else c
   *
   * This method returns true for "a", and false for "b" and "c": the
   * IF expression does something special based on "a"'s value.  "b"
   * and "c" are effectively outputs.  Same logic applies to FOR,
   * WHILE and DO loop predicates.  AND/OR/HOOK expressions are
   * syntactic sugar for IF statements; therefore this method returns
   * true for the predicate and false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
1799 |   private boolean valueConsumedByParent(Node n, Node parent) {
1800 |     if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent)) {
1801 |       return parent.getLastChild() == n;
1802 |     }
1803 | 
1804 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1805 |       case Token.NAME:
1806 |       case Token.RETURN:
1807 |         return true;
1808 |       case Token.AND:
1809 |       case Token.OR:
1810 |       case Token.HOOK:
1811 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n;
1812 |       case Token.FOR:
1813 |         return parent.getFirstChild().getNext() == n;
1814 |       case Token.IF:
1815 |       case Token.WHILE:
1816 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n;
1817 |       case Token.DO:
1818 |         return parent.getLastChild() == n;
1819 |       default:
1820 |         return false;
1821 |     }
1822 |   }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.getThreadTrace()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 958-966
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the ThreadTrace for the current thread, creating one if necessary.
   */
Method code:
958 |   static ThreadTrace getThreadTrace() {
959 |     ThreadTrace t = traces.get();
960 |     if (t == null) {

===== M0428 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1575-1590
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1575 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1576 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1577 |       String name = getString();
1578 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1579 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1580 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1581 |       if (left == null) {
1582 |         return null;
1583 |       }
1584 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1585 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1586 |       return "this";
1587 |     } else {
1588 |       return null;
1589 |     }
1590 |   }

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

Original range: 422-452

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
422 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
423 |       Preconditions.checkState(
424 |           n.isName()
425 |           || n.isString()
426 |           || n.isStringKey()
427 |           || n.isGetterDef()
428 |           || n.isSetterDef());
429 | 
430 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
431 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
432 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
433 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n);
434 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
435 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
436 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
437 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
438 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
439 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
440 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
441 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
442 |           if (assertOnChange) {
443 |             String name = n.getString();
444 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
445 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
446 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
447 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
448 |           }
449 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }

===== M0448 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindReferences.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 803-850
Documentation:

Method code:
803 |     @Override
804 |     public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
805 |       if (parent == null) {
806 |         return true;
807 |       }
808 | 
809 |       // Gather the list of nodes that either have side effects, are
810 |       // arguments to function calls with side effects or are used in
811 |       // control structure predicates.  These names are always
812 |       // referenced when the enclosing function is called.
813 |       if (n.isFor()) {
814 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
815 |           Node decl = n.getFirstChild();
816 |           Node pred = decl.getNext();
817 |           Node step = pred.getNext();
818 |           addSimplifiedExpression(decl, n);
819 |           addSimplifiedExpression(pred, n);
820 |           addSimplifiedExpression(step, n);
821 |         } else { // n.getChildCount() == 3
822 |           Node decl = n.getFirstChild();
823 |           Node iter = decl.getNext();
824 |           addAllChildren(decl);
825 |           addAllChildren(iter);
826 |         }
827 |       }
828 | 
829 |       if (parent.isVar() ||
830 |           parent.isExprResult() ||
831 |           parent.isReturn() ||
832 |           parent.isThrow()) {
833 |         addSimplifiedExpression(n, parent);
834 |       }
835 | 
836 |       if ((parent.isIf() ||
837 |            parent.isWhile() ||
838 |            parent.isWith() ||
839 |            parent.isSwitch() ||
840 |            parent.isCase()) &&
841 |           parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
842 |         addAllChildren(n);
843 |       }
844 | 
845 |       if (parent.isDo() && parent.getLastChild() == n) {
846 |         addAllChildren(n);
847 |       }
848 | 
849 |       return true;
850 |     }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.referenceAliases()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1354-1363
Documentation:
/**
   * The NameAnalyzer algorithm works best when all objects have a canonical
   * name in the global scope. When multiple names in the global scope
   * point to the same object, things start to break down.
   *
   * For example, if we have
   * <code>
   * var a = {};
   * var b = a;
   * a.foo = 3;
   * alert(b.foo);
   * </code>
   * then a.foo and b.foo are the same name, even though NameAnalyzer doesn't
   * represent them as such.
   *
   * To handle this case, we look at all the aliases in the program.
   * If descendant properties of that alias are assigned, then we create a
   * directional reference from the original name to the alias. For example,
   * in this case, the assign to {@code a.foo} triggers a reference from
   * {@code b} to {@code a}, but NOT from a to b.
   *
   * Similarly, "instanceof" checks do not prevent the removal
   * of a unaliased name but an instanceof check on an alias can only be removed
   * if the other aliases are also removed, so we add a connection here.
   */
Method code:
1354 |   private void referenceAliases() {
1355 |     for (Map.Entry<String, AliasSet> entry : aliases.entrySet()) {
1356 |       JsName name = getName(entry.getKey(), false);
1357 |       if (name.hasWrittenDescendants || name.hasInstanceOfReference) {
1358 |         for (String alias : entry.getValue().names) {
1359 |           recordReference(alias, entry.getKey(), RefType.REGULAR);
1360 |         }
1361 |       }
1362 |     }
1363 |   }

168 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 624-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
624 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
625 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
626 |         next = c.getNext();
627 |         if (c.isVar()) {
628 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
629 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
630 |           }
631 | 
632 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
633 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
634 |             c.removeChild(name);
635 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
636 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
637 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
638 |           }
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
452 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
453 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
454 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
455 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
456 | 
457 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
458 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
459 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
460 |

462 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
463 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
464 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
465 |     }

468 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
469 |       if (superClass != null) {
470 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
471 |       }

475 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
476 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
477 |           }

479 |       }
480 |     }

483 |       clearCachedValues();
484 |     }
485 |

775 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
776 |     boolean valid = false;

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindDependencyScopes.recordConsumers(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 530-556
Documentation:

Method code:
530 |     private void recordConsumers(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node recordNode) {
531 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
532 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
533 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
534 |           if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
535 |             recordAssignment(t, parent, recordNode);
536 |           }
537 |           recordConsumers(t, parent, recordNode);
538 |           break;
539 |         case Token.NAME:
540 |           NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, parent);
541 |           recordDepScope(recordNode, ns);
542 |           break;
543 |         case Token.OR:
544 |           recordConsumers(t, parent, recordNode);
545 |           break;
546 |         case Token.AND:
547 |           // In "a && b" only "b" can be meaningfully aliased.
548 |           // "a" must be falsy, which it must be an immutable, non-Object
549 |         case Token.COMMA:
550 |         case Token.HOOK:
551 |           if (n != parent.getFirstChild()) {
552 |             recordConsumers(t, parent, recordNode);
553 |           }
554 |           break;
555 |       }
556 |     }

===== M0534 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 946-1005
Documentation:

Method code:
946 |   private void toString(
947 |       StringBuilder sb,
948 |       boolean printSource,
949 |       boolean printAnnotations,
950 |       boolean printType) {
951 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
952 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
953 |       sb.append(' ');
954 |       sb.append(getString());
955 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
956 |       sb.append(' ');
957 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
958 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
959 |       // toStringTree.
960 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
961 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
962 |       } else {
963 |         sb.append(first.getString());
964 |       }
965 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
966 |       sb.append(' ');
967 |       sb.append(getDouble());
968 |     }
969 |     if (printSource) {
970 |       int lineno = getLineno();
971 |       if (lineno != -1) {
972 |         sb.append(' ');
973 |         sb.append(lineno);
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 | 
977 |     if (printAnnotations) {
978 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
979 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
980 |         int type = keys[i];
981 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
982 |         sb.append(" [");
983 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
984 |         sb.append(": ");
985 |         String value;
986 |         switch (type) {
987 |           default:
988 |             value = x.toString();
989 |             break;
990 |         }
991 |         sb.append(value);
992 |         sb.append(']');
993 |       }
994 |     }
995 | 
996 |     if (printType) {
997 |       if (jsType != null) {
998 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
999 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1000 |           sb.append(" : ");
1001 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1002 |         }
1003 |       }
1004 |     }
1005 |   }

===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1091-1112
Documentation:

Method code:
1091 |   static boolean nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node n, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1092 |     if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
1093 |       return true;
1094 |     }
1095 | 
1096 |     switch(n.getType()) {
1097 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1098 |       case Token.DEC:
1099 |       case Token.INC:
1100 |       case Token.THROW:
1101 |         return true;
1102 |       case Token.CALL:
1103 |         return NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1104 |       case Token.NEW:

1106 |       case Token.NAME:
1107 |         // A variable definition.
1108 |         return n.hasChildren();
1109 |       default:
1110 |         return false;
1111 |     }
1112 |   }

===== M0558 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.

107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();

114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);

134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {

137 |     } finally {

/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
162 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
163 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
164 |     String methodName = null;
165 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
166 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
167 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
168 |       String name = callName.getString();
169 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
170 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
171 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 | 
175 |     if (methodName != null) {
176 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
177 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
178 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
179 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
180 |       }
181 |     }
182 |     return null;
183 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1734-1782
Documentation:
/**
   * Simplify a toplevel expression, while preserving program
   * behavior.
   */
Method code:
1734 |   private void replaceTopLevelExpressionWithRhs(Node parent, Node n) {
1735 |     // validate inputs
1736 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1737 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1738 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1739 |       case Token.FOR:
1740 |       case Token.LABEL:
1741 |         break;
1742 |       default:
1743 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1744 |             "Unsupported parent node type in replaceWithRhs " +
1745 |             Token.name(parent.getType()));
1746 |     }
1747 | 
1748 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1749 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
1750 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1751 |       case Token.VAR:
1752 |         break;
1753 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1754 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(parent.isFor(),
1755 |             "Unsupported assignment in replaceWithRhs. parent: %s",
1756 |             Token.name(parent.getType()));
1757 |         break;
1758 |       default:
1759 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1760 |             "Unsupported node type in replaceWithRhs " +
1761 |             Token.name(n.getType()));
1762 |     }
1763 | 
1764 |     // gather replacements
1765 |     List<Node> replacements = Lists.newArrayList();
1766 |     for (Node rhs : getRhsSubexpressions(n)) {
1767 |       replacements.addAll(getSideEffectNodes(rhs));
1768 |     }
1769 | 
1770 |     if (parent.isFor()) {
1771 |       // tweak replacements array s.t. it is a single expression node.
1772 |       if (replacements.isEmpty()) {
1773 |         replacements.add(IR.empty());
1774 |       } else {
1775 |         Node expr = collapseReplacements(replacements);
1776 |         replacements.clear();
1777 |         replacements.add(expr);
1778 |       }
1779 |     }
1780 | 
1781 |     changeProxy.replaceWith(parent, n, replacements);
1782 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0577 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GatherSideEffectSubexpressionsCallback.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GatherSideEffectSubexpressionsCallback.java
Original range: 227-266
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the list of subtrees whose root nodes have side effects.
   *
   * <p>If the current subtree's root has side effects this method should
   * call accumulator.keepSubTree and return 'false' to add the
   * subtree to the result list and avoid avoid traversing the nodes children.
   *
   * <p>Branching nodes whose then or else branch contain side effects
   * must be simplified by doing a recursive traversal; this method
   * should call the appropriate accumulator 'keepSimplified' method
   * and return 'false' to stop the regular traversal.
   */
Method code:
227 |   @Override
228 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
229 |       NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
230 |     if (FORBIDDEN_TYPES.contains(node.getType()) ||
231 |         NodeUtil.isControlStructure(node)) {
232 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
233 |           Token.name(node.getType()) + " nodes are not supported.");
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     // Do not recurse into nested functions.
237 |     if (node.isFunction()) {
238 |       return false;
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     // simplify and maybe keep hook expression.
242 |     if (node.isHook()) {
243 |       return processHook(node);
244 |     }
245 | 
246 |     // simplify and maybe keep AND/OR expression.
247 |     if ((node.isAnd()) || (node.isOr())) {
248 |       return processShortCircuitExpression(node);
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     if (!NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(node, compiler)) {
252 |       return true;
253 |     } else {
254 | 
255 |       // Node type suggests that the expression has side effects.
256 | 
257 |       if (node.isCall()) {
258 |         return processFunctionCall(node);
259 |       } else if (node.isNew()) {
260 |         return processConstructorCall(node);
261 |       } else {
262 |         accumulator.keepSubTree(node);
263 |         return false;
264 |       }
265 |     }
266 |   }

1365 |         }
1366 | 
1367 |         @Override
1368 |         public Node next() {
1369 |           if (!hasNext()) {
1370 |             throw new NoSuchElementException();
1371 |           }
1372 |           Node n = cur;
1373 |           cur = cur.getParent();
1374 |           return n;
1375 |         }
1376 | 
1377 |         @Override
1378 |         public void remove() {
1379 |           throw new UnsupportedOperationException();
1380 |         }
1381 |       };
1382 |     }
