===== M0596 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 548-561
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
548 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
549 |       Node ret;
550 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
551 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
552 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
553 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
554 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
555 |       } else {
556 |         ret = transform(n);
557 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
558 |       }
559 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
560 |       return ret;
561 |     }

   * @param item The item to inspect

Method code:
796 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
797 |     if (item == null) {
798 |       return null;

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 567-614
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private void extractForInitializer(
568 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
569 | 
570 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
571 |         next = c.getNext();
572 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
573 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
574 |         switch (c.getType()) {
575 |           case Token.LABEL:
576 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
577 |             break;
578 |           case Token.FOR:
579 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
580 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
581 |               if (first.isVar()) {
582 |                 // Transform:
583 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
584 |                 // to:
585 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
586 |                 Node newStatement = first;
587 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
588 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
589 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
590 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
591 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
592 |               }
593 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
594 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
595 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
596 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
597 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
598 | 
599 |               Node newStatement;
600 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
601 |               // but are handled differently.
602 |               if (init.isVar()) {
603 |                 newStatement = init;
604 |               } else {
605 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
606 |               }
607 | 
608 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
609 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
610 |             }
611 |             break;
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 |     }

===== M0618 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2965-3031
Documentation:
/**
   * @param locals A predicate to apply to unknown local values.
   * @return Whether the node is known to be a value that is not a reference
   *     outside the expression scope.
   */
Method code:
2965 |   static boolean evaluatesToLocalValue(Node value, Predicate<Node> locals) {
2966 |     switch (value.getType()) {
2967 |       case Token.CAST:
2968 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals);
2969 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
2970 |         // A result that is aliased by a non-local name, is the effectively the
2971 |         // same as returning a non-local name, but this doesn't matter if the
2972 |         // value is immutable.
2973 |         return NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value.getLastChild())
2974 |             || (locals.apply(value)
2975 |                 && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals));
2976 |       case Token.COMMA:
2977 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2978 |       case Token.AND:
2979 |       case Token.OR:
2980 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals)
2981 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2982 |       case Token.HOOK:
2983 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild().getNext(), locals)
2984 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2985 |       case Token.INC:
2986 |       case Token.DEC:
2987 |         if (value.getBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP)) {
2988 |           return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals);
2989 |         } else {
2990 |           return true;
2991 |         }
2992 |       case Token.THIS:
2993 |         return locals.apply(value);
2994 |       case Token.NAME:
2995 |         return isImmutableValue(value) || locals.apply(value);
2996 |       case Token.GETELEM:
2997 |       case Token.GETPROP:
2998 |         // There is no information about the locality of object properties.
2999 |         return locals.apply(value);
3000 |       case Token.CALL:
3001 |         return callHasLocalResult(value)
3002 |             || isToStringMethodCall(value)
3003 |             || locals.apply(value);
3004 |       case Token.NEW:
3005 |         return newHasLocalResult(value)
3006 |                || locals.apply(value);
3007 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
3008 |       case Token.REGEXP:
3009 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
3010 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
3011 |         // Literals objects with non-literal children are allowed.
3012 |         return true;
3013 |       case Token.DELPROP:
3014 |       case Token.IN:
3015 |         // TODO(johnlenz): should IN operator be included in #isSimpleOperator?
3016 |         return true;
3017 |       default:
3018 |         // Other op force a local value:
3019 |         //  x = '' + g (x is now an local string)
3020 |         //  x -= g (x is now an local number)
3021 |         if (isAssignmentOp(value)
3022 |             || isSimpleOperator(value)
3023 |             || isImmutableValue(value)) {
3024 |           return true;
3025 |         }
3026 | 
3027 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
3028 |             "Unexpected expression node" + value +
3029 |             "\n parent:" + value.getParent());
3030 |     }
3031 |   }

===== M0631 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0649 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.createNameInformation(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1401-1500
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates name information for the current node during a traversal.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal
   * @param n The current node
   * @return The name information, or null if the name is irrelevant to this
   *     pass
   */
Method code:
1401 |   private NameInformation createNameInformation(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1402 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1403 |     // Build the full name and find its root node by iterating down through all
1404 |     // GETPROP/GETELEM nodes.
1405 |     String name = "";
1406 |     Node rootNameNode = n;
1407 |     boolean bNameWasShortened = false;
1408 |     while (true) {
1409 |       if (NodeUtil.isGet(rootNameNode)) {
1410 |         Node prop = rootNameNode.getLastChild();
1411 |         if (rootNameNode.isGetProp()) {
1412 |           name = "." + prop.getString() + name;
1413 |         } else {
1414 |           // We consider the name to be "a.b" in a.b['c'] or a.b[x].d.
1415 |           bNameWasShortened = true;
1416 |           name = "";
1417 |         }
1418 |         rootNameNode = rootNameNode.getFirstChild();
1419 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(rootNameNode)) {
1420 |         name = "." + rootNameNode.getString() + name;
1421 | 
1422 |         // Check if this is an object literal assigned to something.
1423 |         Node objLit = rootNameNode.getParent();
1424 |         Node objLitParent = objLit.getParent();
1425 |         if (objLitParent.isAssign()) {
1426 |           // This must be the right side of the assign.
1427 |           rootNameNode = objLitParent.getFirstChild();
1428 |         } else if (objLitParent.isName()) {
1429 |           // This must be a VAR initialization.
1430 |           rootNameNode = objLitParent;
1431 |         } else if (objLitParent.isStringKey()) {
1432 |           // This must be a object literal key initialization.
1433 |           rootNameNode = objLitParent;
1434 |         } else {
1435 |           return null;
1436 |         }
1437 |       } else {
1438 |         break;
1439 |       }
1440 |     }
1441 | 
1442 |     // Check whether this is a class-defining call. Classes may only be defined
1443 |     // in the global scope.
1444 |     if (parent.isCall() && t.inGlobalScope()) {
1445 |       CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
1446 |       SubclassRelationship classes = convention.getClassesDefinedByCall(parent);
1447 |       if (classes != null) {
1448 |         NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1449 |         nameInfo.name = classes.subclassName;
1450 |         nameInfo.onlyAffectsClassDef = true;
1451 |         nameInfo.superclass = classes.superclassName;
1452 |         return nameInfo;
1453 |       }
1454 | 
1455 |       String singletonGetterClass =
1456 |           convention.getSingletonGetterClassName(parent);
1457 |       if (singletonGetterClass != null) {
1458 |         NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1459 |         nameInfo.name = singletonGetterClass;
1460 |         nameInfo.onlyAffectsClassDef = true;
1461 |         return nameInfo;
1462 |       }
1463 |     }
1464 | 
1465 |     switch (rootNameNode.getType()) {
1466 |       case Token.NAME:
1467 |         // Check whether this is an assignment to a prototype property
1468 |         // of an object defined in the global scope.
1469 |         if (!bNameWasShortened &&
1470 |             n.isGetProp() &&
1471 |             parent.isAssign() &&
1472 |             "prototype".equals(n.getLastChild().getString())) {
1473 |           if (createNameInformation(t, n.getFirstChild()) != null) {
1474 |             name = rootNameNode.getString() + name;
1475 |             name = name.substring(0, name.length() - PROTOTYPE_SUFFIX_LEN);
1476 |             NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1477 |             nameInfo.name = name;
1478 |             return nameInfo;
1479 |           } else {
1480 |             return null;
1481 |           }
1482 |         }
1483 |         return createNameInformation(
1484 |             rootNameNode.getString() + name, t.getScope(), rootNameNode);
1485 |       case Token.THIS:
1486 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
1487 |           NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1488 |           if (name.indexOf('.') == 0) {
1489 |             nameInfo.name = name.substring(1);  // strip leading "."
1490 |           } else {
1491 |             nameInfo.name = name;
1492 |           }
1493 |           nameInfo.isExternallyReferenceable = true;
1494 |           return nameInfo;
1495 |         }
1496 |         return null;
1497 |       default:
1498 |         return null;
1499 |     }
1500 |   }

===== M0667 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.JsNameRefNode.remove()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 313-339
Documentation:

Method code:
313 |     @Override
314 |     public void remove() {
315 |       // Setters have VAR, FUNCTION, or ASSIGN parent nodes. CALL parent
316 |       // nodes are global refs, and are handled later in this function.
317 |       Node containingNode = parent.getParent();
318 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
319 |         case Token.VAR:
320 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.hasOneChild());
321 |           replaceWithRhs(containingNode, parent);
322 |           break;
323 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
324 |           replaceWithRhs(containingNode, parent);
325 |           break;
326 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
327 |           if (containingNode.isExprResult()) {
328 |             replaceWithRhs(containingNode.getParent(), containingNode);
329 |           } else {
330 |             replaceWithRhs(containingNode, parent);
331 |           }
332 |           break;
333 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
334 |           // TODO(nicksantos): Come up with a way to remove this.
335 |           // If we remove object lit keys, then we will need to also
336 |           // create dependency scopes for them.
337 |           break;
338 |       }
339 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 766-849
Documentation:

Method code:
766 |     @Override
767 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
768 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
769 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
770 |       if (name == null) {
771 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
772 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
773 |           errorReporter.error(
774 |             "unnamed function statement",
775 |             sourceName,
776 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
777 | 
778 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
779 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
780 |         }
781 |         name = new Name();
782 |         name.setIdentifier("");
783 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
784 |       }
785 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
786 |       // if the function has an inline return annotation, attach it
787 |       Node newName = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(name);
788 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
789 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
790 |         // declaration.
791 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
792 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
793 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
794 |         // same line as the function keyword.
795 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
796 |             functionNode.getLp();
797 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
798 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
799 |       }
800 | 
801 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
802 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
803 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
804 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
805 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
806 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
807 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
808 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
809 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
810 |       if (fnName != null) {
811 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
812 |       } else {
813 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
814 |       }
815 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
816 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
817 | 
818 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
819 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
820 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
821 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
822 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
823 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
824 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
825 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
826 |         } else {
827 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
828 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
829 |           // (an error has already been reported).
830 |           Preconditions.checkState(
831 |               config.isIdeMode
832 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
833 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
834 |         }
835 |       }
836 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
837 | 
838 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
839 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
840 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
841 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
842 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
843 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
844 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
845 |       }
846 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
847 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
848 |      return node;
849 |     }

===== M0684 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 647-679
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
647 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
648 |       Preconditions.checkState(
649 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
650 |       Node previous = null;
651 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
652 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
653 |       // are already in the right place.
654 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
655 |         previous = current;
656 |         current = current.getNext();
657 |       }
658 | 
659 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
660 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
661 |       while (current != null) {
662 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
663 |         Node next = current.getNext();
664 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
665 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
666 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
667 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
668 | 
669 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
670 |           // previous declarations).
671 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
672 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
673 |         } else {
674 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
675 |           previous = current;
676 |         }
677 |         current = next;
678 |       }
679 |     }

===== M0691 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1514-1565
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1514 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1515 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1516 |     if (type != node.getType()
1517 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1518 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1519 |       return false;
1520 |     }
1521 | 
1522 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1523 |       return false;
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1527 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1528 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1529 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1530 |         return false;
1531 |       }
1532 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1533 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1534 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1535 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1536 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1537 |           return false;
1538 |         }
1539 |       }
1540 | 
1541 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1542 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1543 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1544 |         return false;
1545 |       }
1546 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1547 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1548 |         return false;
1549 |       }
1550 |     }
1551 | 
1552 |     if (recur) {
1553 |       Node n, n2;
1554 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1555 |            n != null;
1556 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1557 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1558 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1559 |           return false;
1560 |         }
1561 |       }
1562 |     }
1563 | 
1564 |     return true;
1565 |   }

/**
   * Creates name information for a particular qualified name that occurs in a
   * particular scope.
   *
   * @param name A qualified name (e.g. "x" or "a.b.c")
   * @param scope The scope in which {@code name} occurs
   * @param rootNameNode The NAME node for the first token of {@code name}
   * @return The name information, or null if the name is irrelevant to this
   *     pass
   */
Method code:
1512 |   private NameInformation createNameInformation(
1513 |       String name, Scope scope, Node rootNameNode) {
1514 |     // Check the scope. Currently we're only looking at globally scoped vars.
1515 |     String rootName = rootNameNode.getString();
1516 |     Var v = scope.getVar(rootName);
1517 |     boolean isExtern = (v == null && externalNames.contains(rootName));
1518 |     boolean isGlobalRef = (v != null && v.isGlobal()) || isExtern ||
1519 |         rootName.equals(WINDOW);
1520 |     if (!isGlobalRef) {
1521 |       return null;
1522 |     }
1523 | 
1524 |     NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1525 | 
1526 |     // If a prototype property or method, fill in prototype information.
1527 |     int idx = name.indexOf(PROTOTYPE_SUBSTRING);
1528 |     if (idx != -1) {
1529 |       nameInfo.isPrototype = true;
1530 |       nameInfo.prototypeClass = name.substring(0, idx);
1531 |       nameInfo.prototypeProperty = name.substring(
1532 |           idx + PROTOTYPE_SUBSTRING_LEN);
1533 |     }
1534 | 
1535 |     nameInfo.name = name;
1536 |     nameInfo.isExternallyReferenceable =
1537 |         isExtern || isExternallyReferenceable(scope, name);
1538 |     return nameInfo;
1539 |   }

===== M0698 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
170 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
175 |       if (first.isName() &&
176 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
177 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
178 |       }
179 |     }

===== M0699 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0704 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3087-3115
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3087 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3088 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3089 |     if (info == null) {
3090 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3091 |       if (parent == null) {
3092 |         return null;
3093 |       }
3094 | 
3095 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3096 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3097 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3098 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3099 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3100 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3101 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3102 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3103 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3104 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3105 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3106 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3107 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3108 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3109 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3110 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3111 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3112 |       }
3113 |     }
3114 |     return info;
3115 |   }

84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null ||
97 |         // Most passes try to report as many errors as possible,
98 |         // so there may already be errors. We only care if there were
99 |         // errors in the code we just parsed.
100 |         (compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount() > startErrorCount && !compiler.isIdeMode())) {
101 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
102 |       root = IR.script();
103 |     } else {
104 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
108 |     // the source file and module.
109 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
110 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MarkNoSideEffectCalls.GatherNoSideEffectFunctions.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MarkNoSideEffectCalls.java
Original range: 119-159
Documentation:

Method code:
119 |     @Override
120 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
121 |       if (!inExterns && hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
122 |         traversal.report(node, INVALID_NO_SIDE_EFFECT_ANNOTATION);
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       if (node.isGetProp()) {
126 |         if (parent.isExprResult() &&
127 |             hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
128 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.add(node);
129 |         }
130 |       } else if (node.isFunction()) {
131 | 
132 |         // The annotation may attached to the function node, the
133 |         // variable declaration or assignment expression.
134 |         boolean hasAnnotation = hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node);
135 |         List<Node> nameNodes = Lists.newArrayList();
136 |         nameNodes.add(node.getFirstChild());
137 | 
138 |         if (parent.isName()) {
139 |           Node gramp = parent.getParent();
140 |           if (gramp.isVar() &&
141 |               gramp.hasOneChild() &&
142 |               hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(gramp)) {
143 |             hasAnnotation = true;
144 |           }
145 | 
146 |           nameNodes.add(parent);
147 |         } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
148 |           if (hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(parent)) {
149 |             hasAnnotation = true;
150 |           }
151 | 
152 |           nameNodes.add(parent.getFirstChild());
153 |         }
154 | 
155 |         if (hasAnnotation) {
156 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.addAll(nameNodes);
157 |         }
158 |       }
159 |     }

===== M0755 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 72-107
Documentation:

Method code:
72 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
73 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
74 |       if (inputId == null) {
75 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
76 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
77 |         // from the AST.
78 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
79 |       }
80 | 
81 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
82 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
83 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
84 | 
85 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
86 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
87 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
88 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
89 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       // Args: Declare function variables
93 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
94 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
95 |            a = a.getNext()) {
96 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
97 |         declareVar(a);
98 |       }
99 | 
100 |       // Body
101 |       scanVars(body);
102 |     } else {
103 |       // It's the global block
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
105 |       scanVars(n);
106 |     }
107 |   }

Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {

219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());

223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0765 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 136-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
136 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
137 |     if (node.isName()) {
138 |       String name = node.getString();
139 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
140 |         return name;
141 |       } else {
142 |         return null;
143 |       }
144 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
145 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
146 |     }
147 |     return null;
148 |   }

===== M0766 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindDependencyScopes.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 503-528
Documentation:

Method code:
503 |     @Override
504 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
505 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope()) {
506 |         return;
507 |       }
508 | 
509 |       if (n.isAssign()) {
510 |         recordAssignment(t, n, n);

512 |           recordConsumers(t, n, n);

===== M0772 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0783 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1429-1465
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1429 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1430 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1431 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1432 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1433 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1434 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1435 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1436 |           continue;
1437 |         }
1438 |       }
1439 | 
1440 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1441 | 
1442 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1443 |       if (n == null) {
1444 |         continue;
1445 |       }
1446 | 
1447 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1448 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1449 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1450 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1451 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1452 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1453 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1454 | 
1455 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1456 | 
1457 |         externs.add(input);
1458 |         staleInputs = true;
1459 |       }
1460 |     }
1461 | 
1462 |     if (staleInputs) {
1463 |       repartitionInputs();
1464 |     }
1465 |   }

===== M0789 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

/**
   * Checks whether a name can be referenced outside of the compiled code.
   * These names will be the root of dependency trees.
   *
   * @param scope The current variable scope
   * @param name The name
   * @return True if can be referenced outside

561 |       NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, nameNode);
562 |       if (ns != null) {
563 |         if (parent.isFor() && !NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
564 |           // Patch for assignments that appear in the init,
565 |           // condition or iteration part of a FOR loop.  Without
566 |           // this change, all 3 of those parts try to claim the for
567 |           // loop as their dependency scope.  The last assignment in
568 |           // those three fields wins, which can result in incorrect
569 |           // reference edges between referenced and assigned variables.
570 |           //
571 |           // TODO(user) revisit the dependency scope calculation
572 |           // logic.
573 |           if (parent.getFirstChild().getNext() != n) {
574 |             recordDepScope(recordNode, ns);
575 |           } else {
576 |             recordDepScope(nameNode, ns);
577 |           }
578 |         } else {
579 |           // The rhs of the assignment is the caller, so it's used by the
580 |           // context. Don't associate it w/ the lhs.
581 |           // FYI: this fixes only the specific case where the assignment is the
582 |           // caller expression, but it could be nested deeper in the caller and
583 |           // we would still get a bug.
584 |           // See testAssignWithCall2 for an example of this.
585 |           recordDepScope(recordNode, ns);
586 |         }
587 |       }
588 |     }

===== M0803 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindReferences.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 852-906
Documentation:

Method code:
852 |     @Override
853 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
854 |       if (!(n.isName() || (NodeUtil.isGet(n) && !parent.isGetProp()))) {
855 |         // This is not a simple or qualified name.
856 |         return;
857 |       }
858 | 
859 |       NameInformation nameInfo = createNameInformation(t, n);
860 |       if (nameInfo == null) {
861 |         // The name is not a global name
862 |         return;
863 |       }
864 | 
865 |       if (nameInfo.onlyAffectsClassDef) {
866 |         if (nameInfo.superclass != null) {
867 |           recordReference(
868 |               nameInfo.name, nameInfo.superclass, RefType.INHERITANCE);
869 |         }
870 | 
871 |         // Make sure that we record a reference to the function that does
872 |         // the inheritance, so that the inherits() function itself does
873 |         // not get stripped.
874 |         String nodeName = n.getQualifiedName();
875 |         if (nodeName != null) {
876 |           recordReference(
877 |               nameInfo.name, nodeName, RefType.REGULAR);
878 |         }
879 | 
880 |         return;
881 |       }
882 | 
883 |       if (parent.isInstanceOf() &&
884 |           parent.getLastChild() == n &&
885 |           // Don't cover GETELEMs with a global root node.
886 |           n.isQualifiedName()) {
887 |         JsName checkedClass = getName(nameInfo.name, true);
888 |         refNodes.add(new InstanceOfCheckNode(checkedClass, n));
889 |         checkedClass.hasInstanceOfReference = true;
890 |         return;
891 |       }
892 | 
893 |       // Determine which name might be potentially referring to this one by
894 |       // looking up the nearest enclosing dependency scope. It's unnecessary to
895 |       // determine all enclosing dependency scopes because this callback should
896 |       // create a chain of references between them.
897 |       List<NameInformation> referers = getDependencyScope(n);
898 |       if (referers.isEmpty()) {
899 |         maybeRecordReferenceOrAlias(t, n, parent, nameInfo, null);
900 |       } else {
901 |         for (NameInformation referring : referers) {
902 |           maybeRecordReferenceOrAlias(t, n, parent, nameInfo, referring);
903 |         }
904 |         recordAliases(referers);
905 |       }
906 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1176-1190
Documentation:
/**
   * Records a reference from one name to another name.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   private void recordReference(String fromName, String toName,
1177 |                                RefType depType) {
1178 |     if (fromName.equals(toName)) {
1179 |       // Don't bother recording self-references.
1180 |       return;
1181 |     }
1182 | 
1183 |     JsName from = getName(fromName, true);
1184 |     JsName to = getName(toName, true);
1185 |     referenceGraph.createNode(from);
1186 |     referenceGraph.createNode(to);
1187 |     if (!referenceGraph.isConnectedInDirection(from, depType, to)) {
1188 |       referenceGraph.connect(from, depType, to);
1189 |     }
1190 |   }
